     521            3 : TEST_CASE("SubstructuringSolver reproduces full-domain magnetostatic energy",
     522              :           "[substructure][Serial][Parallel]")
     523              : {
     524              :   // Gauge-free H(curl) magnetostatics: pure curl-curl (singular). Fields are
     525              :   // gauge-ambiguous, but the magnetic energy is gauge-invariant. A divergence-free
     526              :   // (rotational) current source is a valid magnetostatic excitation; the region-condensed
     527              :   // energy must match a monolith AMS-singular (min-norm) solve of the identical pure
     528              :   // curl-curl operator.

     555              :   {
     556         3456 :     j.SetSize(3);
     557         3456 :     j(0) = -(x(1) - 0.5);
     558         3456 :     j(1) = (x(0) - 0.5);
     559         3456 :     j(2) = 0.0;
     560         3456 :   };
     561            3 :   mfem::VectorFunctionCoefficient jc(3, jfun);
     562              :   mfem::ParLinearForm lf(&pfes);
     563            3 :   lf.AddDomainIntegrator(new mfem::VectorFEDomainLFIntegrator(jc));
     564            3 :   lf.Assemble();
     565              :   Vector f(pfes.GetTrueVSize());
     566            3 :   lf.ParallelAssemble(f);
     567              : 
     568            3 :   Vector u = ss.SolveSource(f);
     569              :   const double e_sub = Energy(ss, u);  // 1/2 u^T K_curlcurl u (magnetic energy)
     570              : 
     571              :   // Monolith reference with the identical approach: solve curl-curl + small mass (definite,
     572              :   // AMS-convergent), measure energy with the pure curl-curl operator.

     574              :   mfem::Vector nu_by_attr(max_attr), mass_by_attr(max_attr);
     575            3 :   nu_by_attr = 0.0;

     577            3 :   nu_by_attr(0) = 1.0 / mu_r;
     578            3 :   nu_by_attr(1) = 1.0 / mu_e;
     579            3 :   mass_by_attr(0) = 1.0e-3;  // match SubstructuringSolver::Impl::kMagRegularization
     580            3 :   mass_by_attr(1) = 1.0e-3;

     582            3 :   mfem::ParBilinearForm asolve(&pfes);
     583            3 :   asolve.AddDomainIntegrator(new mfem::CurlCurlIntegrator(nu));
     584            3 :   asolve.AddDomainIntegrator(new mfem::VectorFEMassIntegrator(massc));
     585            3 :   asolve.Assemble();
     586            3 :   asolve.Finalize();
     587              :   std::unique_ptr<mfem::HypreParMatrix> Ksolve(asolve.ParallelAssemble());
     588            3 :   mfem::ParBilinearForm aenergy(&pfes);
     589            3 :   aenergy.AddDomainIntegrator(new mfem::CurlCurlIntegrator(nu));
     590            3 :   aenergy.Assemble();
     591            3 :   aenergy.Finalize();
     592              :   std::unique_ptr<mfem::HypreParMatrix> Kpure(aenergy.ParallelAssemble());
     593            3 :   mfem::HypreAMS ams(*Ksolve, &pfes);
     594            3 :   ams.SetPrintLevel(0);
     595            3 :   mfem::HyprePCG pcg(*Ksolve);
     596            3 :   pcg.SetTol(1e-13);
     597            3 :   pcg.SetMaxIter(2000);
     598            3 :   pcg.SetPrintLevel(0);
     599            3 :   pcg.SetPreconditioner(ams);
     600              :   Vector u_full(pfes.GetTrueVSize());
     601            3 :   u_full = 0.0;
     602            3 :   pcg.Mult(f, u_full);
     603              :   Vector t(pfes.GetTrueVSize());
     604            3 :   Kpure->Mult(u_full, t);
     605            3 :   double local = 0.0;
     606         1767 :   for (int i = 0; i < pfes.GetTrueVSize(); i++)
     607              :   {
     608         1764 :     local += u_full(i) * t(i);
     609              :   }

     611            3 :   MPI_Allreduce(&local, &e_mono, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, Mpi::World());

     613              :

     615            3 :   CHECK(std::abs(e_sub - e_mono) <=
     616              :         1.0e-6 * std::abs(e_mono));  // substructuring == monolith (same operator)

    2858              :   // x(ω) = Σ_k r_k / (ω - p_k) + c_0 + c_1 ω on [1, 2], with two near-real poles in the
    2859              :   // band and one outside it: greedy sampling at the least denominator converges with a few
    2860              :   // samples, interpolates them, and the interpolant matches x between them. (Once the fit

    2867              :   {

    2869          200 :     return static_cast<double>(seed) / 4294967296.0 - 0.5;

    2874              :     {
    2875          200 :       v->emplace_back(rnd(), rnd());

    2886       122280 :         y[q] += r[k][q] / (omega - p[k]);
    2887              :       }
    2888              :     }

    2895        41697 :     for (std::size_t q = 0; q < a.size(); q++)
    2896              :     {
    2897        40680 :       d += std::norm(a[q] - b[q]);
    2898        40680 :       m += std::norm(b[q]);
    2899              :     }
    2900         1017 :     return std::sqrt(d / m);
